Estuda o estado do produto (docs/sdd/tasks.md, specification.md, technical-plan.md, CLAUDE.md), evita duplicar o que já existe (issues abertas + tasks concluídas), e escreve issues bem-formadas que o `feature-spec` consegue consumir direto. Não escreve código nem spec técnica, e NÃO propõe trabalho técnico interno (refactor, infra, dívida, cobertura de teste) — decide O QUÊ evolui o valor do produto para as personas, ORIENTADO POR BENCHM… ([celfons/ai-first](https://github.com/celfons/ai-first/blob/HEAD/agents/product-owner.md) · ★1)
- **product-owner**: Product owner and roadmap keeper. Use proactively for roadmap planning, work sequencing, phase prioritization, capturing strategic direction (vs. setting strategic direction), feature scoping, dependency analysis, or evaluating whether proposed work aligns with the project trajectory. Consult BEFORE starting non-trivial feature work and AFTER completing significant work. ([cpliakas/claude-code-digital-coworkers](https://github.com/cpliakas/claude-code-digital-coworkers/blob/HEAD/plugins/product-ops/agents/product-owner.md) · ★1)
- **product-owner**: 收斂客戶、行銷、資料分析師的訴求，挑出最有價值的方向，寫成標準 INVEST 格式的 User Story 含 AC。是 stakeholder 與技術團隊之間的橋樑。Use when synthesizing stakeholder input into User Stories, prioritizing backlog, or defining acceptance criteria. ([easylive1989/lorescape](https://github.com/easylive1989/lorescape/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/product-owner.md) · ★1)
- **product-owner**: Product Owner — User value perspective, participates in two-round discussions (spawn on demand) ([fulcrumaxe/fulcrumaxe](https://github.com/fulcrumaxe/fulcrumaxe/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/product-owner.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **product-owner**: MULTI-PHASE ORCHESTRATOR. Reads PRD/TDD documents and coordinates specialist agents. Protocol: 1) "PHASE 1: Parse [prd-path] [tdd-path?]" → analyze → present task breakdown → AskUserQuestion (approve/modify). 2) "PHASE 2: Approved breakdown: [tasks]. Generate delegation prompts." → output ready-to-use prompts for each agent → AskUserQuestion (start / reorder / skip). 3) "PHASE 3: Execute" → present first task prompt → user invokes agent → user reports back → mark complete → present next. CRITICAL: Never skip user approval between phases. Use TodoWrite to track progress across agent switches. ([JanSzewczyk/claude-plugins](https://github.com/JanSzewczyk/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/product-management/agents/product-owner.md) · ★1)
- **product-owner**: Product Owner: формулирует ценность и бизнес-цели, пишет User Story с Acceptance Criteria, приоритизирует backlog по RICE, WSJF или MoSCoW, считает бизнес-кейс и ROI, ведёт роадмап и карту стейкхолдеров. Используй, когда нужно «написать user story», «критерии приёмки», «приоритизировать backlog», «посчитать ROI или бизнес-кейс», «роадмап», «кто стейкхолдеры». Процессы и BRD делает business-analyst, спецификации делает system-analyst. ([jtprogru/bear-skills](https://github.com/jtprogru/bear-skills/blob/HEAD/domains/analysis/agents/product-owner.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **product-owner**: Backlog manager and Sprint goal definer responsible for story selection, priority decisions, and stakeholder communication ([kctw-dev/shikigami](https://github.com/kctw-dev/shikigami/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/product-owner.md) · ★1)
- **product-owner**: 在需求定義、優先級決策、Sprint 規劃、Backlog 管理時調度此 Agent ([kctw-dev/shikigami](https://github.com/kctw-dev/shikigami/blob/HEAD/agents/product-owner.md) · ★1)

([nexaedge/nexaedge-marketplace](https://github.com/nexaedge/nexaedge-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/linear-spec-plugin/agents/product-owner.md) · ★1)
- **product-owner**: Product manager and tech lead. Breaks versions into executable stories, then stays live the whole session: answers engineer questions, re-refines scope, consolidates lessons, and runs the final spec review. ([nexaedge/nexaedge-marketplace](https://github.com/nexaedge/nexaedge-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/spec-plugin/agents/product-owner.md) · ★1)
- **product-owner**: The product-owner operating contract for the-unseen-hand. This is primarily the role the MAIN SESSION adopts — single point of contact with the human for requirements and decisions, holder of the product vision, and coordinator who delegates implementation to the specialist agents. Sequences issues within milestones if a roadmap exists. Refines vague issues into actionable scope. Invoked as `@product-owner draft an issue for X` or `@product-owner refine issue &lt;num&gt;`. Does NOT write code. ([Sfzmango/Maungs-agentic-toolbelt](https://github.com/Sfzmango/Maungs-agentic-toolbelt/blob/HEAD/agents/product-owner.md) · ★1)
- **product-owner**: The Product Owner manages the product backlog, roadmap, and sprint priorities. They represent stakeholder interests, define acceptance criteria, and ensure the team builds the right things in the right order. Use this agent for backlog grooming, roadmap planning, prioritization decisions, and stakeholder alignment. ([Sincebook/Claude-Code-Software-Company](https://github.com/Sincebook/Claude-Code-Software-Company/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/product-owner.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **product-owner**: Product Owner for Dossier — owns what we build, for whom, why, and in what order. Use to set or challenge scope, prioritize the roadmap, write a crisp spec/acceptance for a feature, define success metrics, or pressure-test whether a piece of work actually ladders to the learning-loop mission. - **product-owner**: Grooms the request into acceptance criteria and a Fibonacci estimate before any code is written. - **product-owner**: Seasoned product owner for on-demand product work in three modes — Shape (interrogate a fuzzy idea into a PRD/spec with acceptance criteria and non-goals), Guard (review a plan, spec, or PR diff against product intent — gaps, YAGNI cuts, verdict), and Cut (split an approved spec into prioritized, ready-for-dev work items on the queue).
